By \paperlink{premise:4966662e6f665f6571}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4966662e6f665f6571}}, we obtain \(a_{1} \in \operatorname{antidiagonal}\left(n\right) \iff \pi_1(a_{1}) + \pi_2(a_{1}) = n\).
By \paperlink{premise:6578697374735f70726f705f636f6e6772}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6578697374735f70726f705f636f6e6772}}, we obtain \((\exists b\text{ proving }(a_{1} \in \operatorname{antidiagonal}(n)),\; \exists a_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \exists b_{1}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (a_{2} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})) \land b_{1} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} a_{2},\allowbreak{} b_{1}) = a) \iff \pi_1(a_{1}) + \pi_2(a_{1}) = n \land (\exists a_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \exists b\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (a_{2} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})) \land b \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} a_{2},\allowbreak{} b) = a)\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\exists b\text{ proving }(a_{1} \in \operatorname{antidiagonal}(n)),\; \exists a_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \exists b_{1}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (a_{2} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})) \land b_{1} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} a_{2},\allowbreak{} b_{1}) = a) = (\pi_1(a_{1}) + \pi_2(a_{1}) = n \land (\exists a_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \exists b\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (a_{2} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})) \land b \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} a_{2},\allowbreak{} b) = a))\).
By \paperlink{premise:6578697374735f70726f70}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6578697374735f70726f70}}, we obtain \(\pi_1(a_{1}) + \pi_2(a_{1}) = n \land (\exists a_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \exists b\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (a_{2} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})) \land b \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} a_{2},\allowbreak{} b) = a) \iff \pi_1(a_{1}) + \pi_2(a_{1}) = n \land (\exists a_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \exists b\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (a_{2} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})) \land b \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} a_{2},\allowbreak{} b) = a)\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\pi_1(a_{1}) + \pi_2(a_{1}) = n \land (\exists a_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \exists b\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (a_{2} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})) \land b \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} a_{2},\allowbreak{} b) = a)) = (\pi_1(a_{1}) + \pi_2(a_{1}) = n \land (\exists a_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \exists b\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (a_{2} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})) \land b \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} a_{2},\allowbreak{} b) = a))\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((\exists b\text{ proving }(a_{1} \in \operatorname{antidiagonal}(n)),\; \exists a_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \exists b_{1}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (a_{2} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})) \land b_{1} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} a_{2},\allowbreak{} b_{1}) = a) = (\pi_1(a_{1}) + \pi_2(a_{1}) = n \land (\exists a_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \exists b\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (a_{2} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})) \land b \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} a_{2},\allowbreak{} b) = a))\).
\end{proofstep}
The substitution uses the following argument.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(a_{1}\) in \(\mathbb{N} \times \mathbb{N}\).
By \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e486173416e7469646961676f6e616c2e6d656d5f616e7469646961676f6e616c}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e486173416e7469646961676f6e616c2e6d656d5f616e7469646961676f6e616c}} with \(A = \mathbb{N}\), we obtain \(a_{1} \in \operatorname{antidiagonal}\left(n\right) \iff \pi_1(a_{1}) + \pi_2(a_{1}) = n\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((a_{1} \in \operatorname{antidiagonal}(n)) = (\pi_1(a_{1}) + \pi_2(a_{1}) = n)\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((y \mapsto a_{1} \in \operatorname{antidiagonal}(n) \land y) = (y \mapsto \pi_1(a_{1}) + \pi_2(a_{1}) = n \land y)\).
By \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f6d6170}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f6d6170}} with \(\alpha = \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \times \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit})\) and \(\beta = \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\), we obtain \(a \in \operatorname{map}(\operatorname{Function\mathord{:}Embedding\mathord{:}mk}(x \mapsto \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x))),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1})))) \iff (\exists a_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \times \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; a_{2} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))) \land \operatorname{coe}(\operatorname{Function\mathord{:}Embedding\mathord{:}mk}(x \mapsto \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x))),\allowbreak{} a_{2}) = a)\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((a \in \operatorname{map}(\operatorname{Function\mathord{:}Embedding\mathord{:}mk}(x \mapsto \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x))),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))))) = (\exists a_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \times \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; a_{2} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))) \land \operatorname{coe}(\operatorname{Function\mathord{:}Embedding\mathord{:}mk}(x \mapsto \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x))),\allowbreak{} a_{2}) = a)\).
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(a_{2}\) in \(\operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right) \times \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\).
By \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f70726f64756374}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f70726f64756374}} with \(\alpha = \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\) and \(\beta = \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\), we obtain \(a_{2} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))) \iff \pi_1(a_{2}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})) \land \pi_2(a_{2}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((a_{2} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1})))) = (\pi_1(a_{2}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})) \land \pi_2(a_{2}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1})))\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((a_{2} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(a_{2}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(a_{2})) = a) = ((\pi_1(a_{2}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})) \land \pi_2(a_{2}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(a_{2}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(a_{2})) = a)\).
\end{proofstep}
By function extensionality, we obtain \((x \mapsto x \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x)) = a) = (x \mapsto (\pi_1(x) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})) \land \pi_2(x) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x)) = a)\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((\exists x\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \times \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; x \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x)) = a) = (\exists x\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \times \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (\pi_1(x) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})) \land \pi_2(x) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x)) = a)\).
By \paperlink{premise:50726f642e657869737473}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:50726f642e657869737473}} with \(\alpha = \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\) and \(\beta = \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\), we obtain \((\exists x\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \times \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (x_{1} \mapsto (\pi_1(x_{1}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})) \land \pi_2(x_{1}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x_{1}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x_{1})) = a)(x)) \iff (\exists a_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \exists b\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (x \mapsto (\pi_1(x) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})) \land \pi_2(x) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x)) = a)((a_{2},\allowbreak{} b)))\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\exists x\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \times \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (\pi_1(x) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})) \land \pi_2(x) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x)) = a) = (\exists a_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \exists b\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (\pi_1((a_{2},\allowbreak{} b)) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})) \land \pi_2((a_{2},\allowbreak{} b)) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1((a_{2},\allowbreak{} b)),\allowbreak{} \pi_2((a_{2},\allowbreak{} b))) = a)\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((\exists x\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \times \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; x \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x)) = a) = (\exists a_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \exists b\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (\pi_1((a_{2},\allowbreak{} b)) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})) \land \pi_2((a_{2},\allowbreak{} b)) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1((a_{2},\allowbreak{} b)),\allowbreak{} \pi_2((a_{2},\allowbreak{} b))) = a)\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((a \in \operatorname{map}(\operatorname{Function\mathord{:}Embedding\mathord{:}mk}(x \mapsto \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x))),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))))) = (\exists a_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \exists b\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (\pi_1((a_{2},\allowbreak{} b)) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})) \land \pi_2((a_{2},\allowbreak{} b)) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1((a_{2},\allowbreak{} b)),\allowbreak{} \pi_2((a_{2},\allowbreak{} b))) = a)\).
By congruence, we obtain \((a_{1} \in \operatorname{antidiagonal}(n) \land a \in \operatorname{map}(\operatorname{Function\mathord{:}Embedding\mathord{:}mk}(x \mapsto \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x))),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))))) = (\pi_1(a_{1}) + \pi_2(a_{1}) = n \land (\exists a_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \exists b\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (\pi_1((a_{2},\allowbreak{} b)) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})) \land \pi_2((a_{2},\allowbreak{} b)) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1((a_{2},\allowbreak{} b)),\allowbreak{} \pi_2((a_{2},\allowbreak{} b))) = a))\).
\end{proofstep}
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f6269556e696f6e}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f6269556e696f6e}}, \paperlink{premise:66756e657874}{\emph{Function extensionality}} (\paperlink{premise:66756e657874}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:66756e657874}}), \paperlink{premise:537562747970652e657869737473}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:537562747970652e657869737473}}, \paperlink{premise:50726f642e657869737473}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:50726f642e657869737473}}, \paperlink{premise:6966665f73656c66}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6966665f73656c66}} and the 3 identities established above gives \(a \in \bigcup_{\mathit{ijh}\in \operatorname{attach}(\operatorname{antidiagonal}(n))} \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(\mathit{ijh})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(\mathit{ijh}))) \iff a \in \bigcup_{\mathit{ij}\in \operatorname{antidiagonal}(n)} \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(\mathit{ij})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(\mathit{ij})))\).
\end{proofstep}
By \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e657874}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e657874}} with \(\alpha = \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\), we obtain \(\bigcup_{\mathit{ijh}\in \operatorname{attach}(\operatorname{antidiagonal}(n))} \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(\mathit{ijh})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(\mathit{ijh}))) = \bigcup_{\mathit{ij}\in \operatorname{antidiagonal}(n)} \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(\mathit{ij})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(\mathit{ij})))\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{defeq:5}{defining equation~D\ref{defeq:5}} in the preceding result gives \(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(n + 1) = \bigcup_{\mathit{ij}\in \operatorname{antidiagonal}(n)} \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(\mathit{ij})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(\mathit{ij})))\).
\end{proof}

\begin{lemma}\label{theorem:11}
Let \(x\) be an element of \(\operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\). Let \(n\) be a natural number. Then
\begin{center}
\(\displaystyle x \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(n) \iff \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x) = n.\)
\end{center}
\end{lemma}

\begin{proof}
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(n_{1}\) in \(\mathbb{N}\).
In the zero case,
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(n_{1} = 0\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{theorem:9}{Lemma~\ref{theorem:9}}, \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f73696e676c65746f6e}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f73696e676c65746f6e}} and \paperlink{premise:6966665f73656c66}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6966665f73656c66}} gives \(\operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}nil} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}\left(0\right) \iff 0 = 0\).
Use the hypothesis \(n_{1} = 0\).
Reversing the equality, we obtain \(0 = n_{1}\).
Substituting equals, we obtain \(\operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}nil} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(n_{1}) \iff \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(\operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}nil}) = n_{1}\).
\end{proofstep}
In the successor case,
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(n_{2}\) in \(\mathbb{N}\).
Assume \(n_{1} = n_{2} + 1\).
The substitution uses the following argument.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(a\) in \(\mathbb{N} \times \mathbb{N}\).
By \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e486173416e7469646961676f6e616c2e6d656d5f616e7469646961676f6e616c}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e486173416e7469646961676f6e616c2e6d656d5f616e7469646961676f6e616c}} with \(A = \mathbb{N}\), we obtain \(a \in \operatorname{antidiagonal}\left(n_{2}\right) \iff \pi_1(a) + \pi_2(a) = n_{2}\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \(\left(a \in \operatorname{antidiagonal}\left(n_{2}\right)\right) = \left(\pi_1(a) + \pi_2(a) = n_{2}\right)\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((y \mapsto a \in \operatorname{antidiagonal}(n_{2}) \land y) = (y \mapsto \pi_1(a) + \pi_2(a) = n_{2} \land y)\).
By \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f6d6170}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f6d6170}} with \(\alpha = \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \times \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit})\) and \(\beta = \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\), we obtain \(\operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}nil} \in \operatorname{map}(\operatorname{Function\mathord{:}Embedding\mathord{:}mk}(x_{1} \mapsto \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x_{1}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x_{1}))),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a)))) \iff (\exists a_{1}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \times \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; a_{1} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a))) \land \operatorname{coe}(\operatorname{Function\mathord{:}Embedding\mathord{:}mk}(x_{1} \mapsto \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x_{1}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x_{1}))),\allowbreak{} a_{1}) = \operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}nil})\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}nil} \in \operatorname{map}(\operatorname{Function\mathord{:}Embedding\mathord{:}mk}(x_{1} \mapsto \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x_{1}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x_{1}))),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a))))) = (\exists a_{1}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \times \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; a_{1} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a))) \land \operatorname{coe}(\operatorname{Function\mathord{:}Embedding\mathord{:}mk}(x_{1} \mapsto \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x_{1}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x_{1}))),\allowbreak{} a_{1}) = \operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}nil})\).
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(a_{1}\) in \(\operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right) \times \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\).
By \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f70726f64756374}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f70726f64756374}} with \(\alpha = \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\) and \(\beta = \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\), we obtain \(a_{1} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a))) \iff \pi_1(a_{1}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a)) \land \pi_2(a_{1}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a))\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((a_{1} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a)))) = (\pi_1(a_{1}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a)) \land \pi_2(a_{1}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a)))\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((y \mapsto a_{1} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a))) \land y) = (y \mapsto (\pi_1(a_{1}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a)) \land \pi_2(a_{1}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a))) \land y)\).
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(\operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(a_{1}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(a_{1})) = \operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}nil}\).
Use the hypothesis \(\operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(a_{1}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(a_{1})) = \operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}nil}\).
By \paperlink{premise:547265652e6e6f436f6e667573696f6e}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:547265652e6e6f436f6e667573696f6e}} with \(\alpha = \operatorname{Unit}\), we obtain \(\operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}no\,Confusion\,Type}(\mathrm{False},\allowbreak{} \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(a_{1}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(a_{1})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}nil})\).
\end{proofstep}
By \paperlink{premise:65715f66616c736527}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:65715f66616c736527}}, we obtain \((\operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(a_{1}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(a_{1})) = \operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}nil}) = \mathrm{False}\).
By congruence, we obtain \((a_{1} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(a_{1}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(a_{1})) = \operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}nil}) = ((\pi_1(a_{1}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a)) \land \pi_2(a_{1}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a))) \land \mathrm{False})\).
By \paperlink{premise:616e645f66616c7365}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:616e645f66616c7365}} with \(p = \pi_1(a_{1}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a)) \land \pi_2(a_{1}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a))\), we obtain \(((\pi_1(a_{1}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a)) \land \pi_2(a_{1}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a))) \land \mathrm{False}) = \mathrm{False}\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((a_{1} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(a_{1}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(a_{1})) = \operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}nil}) = \mathrm{False}\).
\end{proofstep}
By function extensionality, we obtain \((x_{1} \mapsto x_{1} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x_{1}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x_{1})) = \operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}nil}) = (x_{1} \mapsto \mathrm{False})\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((\exists x_{1}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \times \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; x_{1} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x_{1}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x_{1})) = \operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}nil}) = (\exists x_{1}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \times \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \mathrm{False})\).
By \paperlink{premise:696e73744e6f6e656d7074794f66496e68616269746564}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:696e73744e6f6e656d7074794f66496e68616269746564}} with \(\alpha = \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\), we obtain \(\operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\text{ is nonempty}\).
By \paperlink{premise:696e73744e6f6e656d7074794f66496e68616269746564}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:696e73744e6f6e656d7074794f66496e68616269746564}} with \(\alpha = \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\), we obtain \(\operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\text{ is nonempty}\).
By \paperlink{premise:696e73744e6f6e656d70747950726f64}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:696e73744e6f6e656d70747950726f64}} with \(\alpha = \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\) and \(\beta = \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\), we obtain \(\operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \times \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit})\text{ is nonempty}\).
By \paperlink{premise:6578697374735f636f6e7374}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6578697374735f636f6e7374}} with \(\alpha = \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \times \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit})\), we obtain \((\exists x_{1}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \times \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \mathrm{False}) \iff \mathrm{False}\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\exists x_{1}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \times \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \mathrm{False}) = \mathrm{False}\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((\exists x_{1}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \times \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; x_{1} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x_{1}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x_{1})) = \operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}nil}) = \mathrm{False}\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((\operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}nil} \in \operatorname{map}(\operatorname{Function\mathord{:}Embedding\mathord{:}mk}(x_{1} \mapsto \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x_{1}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x_{1}))),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a))))) = \mathrm{False}\).
By congruence, we obtain \((a \in \operatorname{antidiagonal}(n_{2}) \land \operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}nil} \in \operatorname{map}(\operatorname{Function\mathord{:}Embedding\mathord{:}mk}(x_{1} \mapsto \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x_{1}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x_{1}))),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a))))) = (\pi_1(a) + \pi_2(a) = n_{2} \land \mathrm{False})\).
By \paperlink{premise:616e645f66616c7365}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:616e645f66616c7365}} with \(p = \pi_1(a) + \pi_2(a) = n_{2}\), we obtain \(\left(\pi_1(a) + \pi_2(a) = n_{2} \land \mathrm{False}\right) = \mathrm{False}\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((a \in \operatorname{antidiagonal}(n_{2}) \land \operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}nil} \in \operatorname{map}(\operatorname{Function\mathord{:}Embedding\mathord{:}mk}(x_{1} \mapsto \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x_{1}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x_{1}))),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a))))) = \mathrm{False}\).
\end{proofstep}
Rewriting with \paperlink{theorem:10}{Lemma~\ref{theorem:10}}, \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f6269556e696f6e}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f6269556e696f6e}}, \paperlink{premise:66756e657874}{\emph{Function extensionality}} (\paperlink{premise:66756e657874}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:66756e657874}}), \paperlink{premise:6578697374735f636f6e7374}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6578697374735f636f6e7374}}, \paperlink{premise:696e73744e6f6e656d70747950726f64}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:696e73744e6f6e656d70747950726f64}}, \paperlink{premise:696e73744e6f6e656d7074794f66496e68616269746564}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:696e73744e6f6e656d7074794f66496e68616269746564}}, \paperlink{premise:72696768745f65715f616464}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:72696768745f65715f616464}}, \paperlink{premise:4e61742e6164645f65715f7a65726f}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e61742e6164645f65715f7a65726f}}, \paperlink{premise:65715f66616c7365}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:65715f66616c7365}}, \paperlink{premise:6f6e655f6e655f7a65726f}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6f6e655f6e655f7a65726f}}, \paperlink{premise:4e61742e696e73744e655a65726f53756363}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e61742e696e73744e655a65726f53756363}}, \paperlink{premise:616e645f66616c7365}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:616e645f66616c7365}}, \paperlink{premise:6966665f73656c66}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6966665f73656c66}} and the identity established above gives \(\operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}nil} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(n_{2} + 1) \iff 0 = n_{2} + 1 + 0\).
Use the hypothesis \(n_{1} = n_{2} + 1\).
Reversing the equality, we obtain \(n_{2} + 1 = n_{1}\).
Substituting equals, we obtain \(\operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}nil} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(n_{1}) \iff \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(\operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}nil}) = n_{1}\).
\end{proofstep}
By definition, we obtain \(n_{1} = n_{1}\).
The zero and successor cases exhaust the natural numbers. Hence \(\operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}nil} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(n_{1}) \iff \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(\operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}nil}) = n_{1}\).
\end{proofstep}
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(x_{1}\) in \(\operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\).
Fix \(y\) in \(\operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\).
Assume \(\forall n_{1}\, :\, \mathbb{N},\; x_{1} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(n_{1}) \iff \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x_{1}) = n_{1}\).
Assume \(\forall n_{1}\, :\, \mathbb{N},\; y \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(n_{1}) \iff \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(y) = n_{1}\).
Fix \(n_{1}\) in \(\mathbb{N}\).
In the zero case,
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(n_{1} = 0\).
The substitution uses the following argument.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(\operatorname{node}\left(\operatorname{unit}, x_{1}, y\right) = \operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}nil}\).
Use the hypothesis \(\operatorname{node}\left(\operatorname{unit}, x_{1}, y\right) = \operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}nil}\).
By \paperlink{premise:547265652e6e6f436f6e667573696f6e}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:547265652e6e6f436f6e667573696f6e}} with \(\alpha = \operatorname{Unit}\), we obtain \(\operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}no\,Confusion\,Type}(\mathrm{False},\allowbreak{} \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} x_{1},\allowbreak{} y),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{Tree\mathord{:}nil})\).
\end{proofstep}
Rewriting with \paperlink{theorem:9}{Lemma~\ref{theorem:9}}, \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f73696e676c65746f6e}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f73696e676c65746f6e}}, \paperlink{premise:65715f66616c736527}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:65715f66616c736527}}, \paperlink{premise:4e61742e6164645f65715f7a65726f}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e61742e6164645f65715f7a65726f}}, \paperlink{premise:65715f66616c7365}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:65715f66616c7365}}, \paperlink{premise:6f6e655f6e655f7a65726f}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6f6e655f6e655f7a65726f}}, \paperlink{premise:4e61742e696e73744e655a65726f53756363}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e61742e696e73744e655a65726f53756363}}, \paperlink{premise:616e645f66616c7365}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:616e645f66616c7365}}, \paperlink{premise:6966665f73656c66}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6966665f73656c66}} and the auxiliary result established above gives \(\operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} x_{1},\allowbreak{} y) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(0) \iff \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x_{1}) + \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(y) + 1 = 0\).
Use the hypothesis \(n_{1} = 0\).
Reversing the equality, we obtain \(0 = n_{1}\).
Substituting equals, we obtain \(\operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} x_{1},\allowbreak{} y) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(n_{1}) \iff \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(\operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} x_{1},\allowbreak{} y)) = n_{1}\).
\end{proofstep}
In the successor case,
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(n_{2}\) in \(\mathbb{N}\).
Assume \(n_{1} = n_{2} + 1\).
The substitution uses the following argument.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(a_{2}\) in \(\mathbb{N} \times \mathbb{N}\).
By \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e486173416e7469646961676f6e616c2e6d656d5f616e7469646961676f6e616c}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e486173416e7469646961676f6e616c2e6d656d5f616e7469646961676f6e616c}} with \(A = \mathbb{N}\), we obtain \(a_{2} \in \operatorname{antidiagonal}\left(n_{2}\right) \iff \pi_1(a_{2}) + \pi_2(a_{2}) = n_{2}\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((a_{2} \in \operatorname{antidiagonal}(n_{2})) = (\pi_1(a_{2}) + \pi_2(a_{2}) = n_{2})\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((y_{1} \mapsto a_{2} \in \operatorname{antidiagonal}(n_{2}) \land y_{1}) = (y_{1} \mapsto \pi_1(a_{2}) + \pi_2(a_{2}) = n_{2} \land y_{1})\).
By \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f6d6170}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f6d6170}} with \(\alpha = \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \times \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit})\) and \(\beta = \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\), we obtain \(\operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} x_{1},\allowbreak{} y) \in \operatorname{map}(\operatorname{Function\mathord{:}Embedding\mathord{:}mk}(x_{2} \mapsto \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x_{2}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x_{2}))),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2})))) \iff (\exists a_{3}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \times \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; a_{3} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))) \land \operatorname{coe}(\operatorname{Function\mathord{:}Embedding\mathord{:}mk}(x_{2} \mapsto \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x_{2}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x_{2}))),\allowbreak{} a_{3}) = \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} x_{1},\allowbreak{} y))\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} x_{1},\allowbreak{} y) \in \operatorname{map}(\operatorname{Function\mathord{:}Embedding\mathord{:}mk}(x_{2} \mapsto \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x_{2}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x_{2}))),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))))) = (\exists a_{3}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \times \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; a_{3} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))) \land \operatorname{coe}(\operatorname{Function\mathord{:}Embedding\mathord{:}mk}(x_{2} \mapsto \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x_{2}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x_{2}))),\allowbreak{} a_{3}) = \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} x_{1},\allowbreak{} y))\).
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(a_{3}\) in \(\operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right) \times \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\).
By \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f70726f64756374}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f70726f64756374}} with \(\alpha = \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\) and \(\beta = \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\), we obtain \(a_{3} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))) \iff \pi_1(a_{3}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land \pi_2(a_{3}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((a_{3} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2})))) = (\pi_1(a_{3}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land \pi_2(a_{3}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2})))\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((y_{1} \mapsto a_{3} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))) \land y_{1}) = (y_{1} \mapsto (\pi_1(a_{3}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land \pi_2(a_{3}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))) \land y_{1})\).
By \paperlink{premise:547265652e6e6f64652e696e6a4571}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:547265652e6e6f64652e696e6a4571}} with \(\alpha = \operatorname{Unit}\) and \(\operatorname{unit}\) in place of its parameter \(a\) and \(\pi_1(a_{3})\) in place of its parameter \(a_{1}\) and \(\pi_2(a_{3})\) in place of its parameter \(a_{2}\) and \(\operatorname{unit}\) in place of its parameter \(a_{3}\) and \(a_{4} = x_{1}\) and \(a_{5} = y\), we obtain \((\operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(a_{3}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(a_{3})) = \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} x_{1},\allowbreak{} y)) = (\operatorname{unit} = \operatorname{unit} \land (\pi_1(a_{3}) = x_{1} \land \pi_2(a_{3}) = y))\).
By \paperlink{premise:65715f73656c66}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:65715f73656c66}} with \(\alpha = \operatorname{Unit}\) and \(\operatorname{unit}\) in place of its parameter \(a\), we obtain \(\left(\operatorname{unit} = \operatorname{unit}\right) = \mathrm{True}\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((\operatorname{unit} = \operatorname{unit} \land (\pi_1(a_{3}) = x_{1} \land \pi_2(a_{3}) = y)) = (\mathrm{True} \land (\pi_1(a_{3}) = x_{1} \land \pi_2(a_{3}) = y))\).
By \paperlink{premise:747275655f616e64}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:747275655f616e64}} with \(p = \pi_1(a_{3}) = x_{1} \land \pi_2(a_{3}) = y\), we obtain \((\mathrm{True} \land (\pi_1(a_{3}) = x_{1} \land \pi_2(a_{3}) = y)) = (\pi_1(a_{3}) = x_{1} \land \pi_2(a_{3}) = y)\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((\operatorname{unit} = \operatorname{unit} \land (\pi_1(a_{3}) = x_{1} \land \pi_2(a_{3}) = y)) = (\pi_1(a_{3}) = x_{1} \land \pi_2(a_{3}) = y)\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((\operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(a_{3}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(a_{3})) = \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} x_{1},\allowbreak{} y)) = (\pi_1(a_{3}) = x_{1} \land \pi_2(a_{3}) = y)\).
By congruence, we obtain \((a_{3} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(a_{3}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(a_{3})) = \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} x_{1},\allowbreak{} y)) = ((\pi_1(a_{3}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land \pi_2(a_{3}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))) \land (\pi_1(a_{3}) = x_{1} \land \pi_2(a_{3}) = y))\).
\end{proofstep}
By function extensionality, we obtain \((x_{2} \mapsto x_{2} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x_{2}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x_{2})) = \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} x_{1},\allowbreak{} y)) = (x_{2} \mapsto (\pi_1(x_{2}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land \pi_2(x_{2}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))) \land (\pi_1(x_{2}) = x_{1} \land \pi_2(x_{2}) = y))\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((\exists x_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \times \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; x_{2} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x_{2}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x_{2})) = \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} x_{1},\allowbreak{} y)) = (\exists x_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \times \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (\pi_1(x_{2}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land \pi_2(x_{2}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))) \land (\pi_1(x_{2}) = x_{1} \land \pi_2(x_{2}) = y))\).
By \paperlink{premise:50726f642e657869737473}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:50726f642e657869737473}} with \(\alpha = \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\) and \(\beta = \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\), we obtain \((\exists x_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \times \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (x_{3} \mapsto (\pi_1(x_{3}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land \pi_2(x_{3}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))) \land (\pi_1(x_{3}) = x_{1} \land \pi_2(x_{3}) = y))(x_{2})) \iff (\exists a_{3}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \exists b\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (x_{2} \mapsto (\pi_1(x_{2}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land \pi_2(x_{2}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))) \land (\pi_1(x_{2}) = x_{1} \land \pi_2(x_{2}) = y))((a_{3},\allowbreak{} b)))\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\exists x_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \times \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (\pi_1(x_{2}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land \pi_2(x_{2}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))) \land (\pi_1(x_{2}) = x_{1} \land \pi_2(x_{2}) = y)) = (\exists a_{3}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \exists b\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (\pi_1((a_{3},\allowbreak{} b)) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land \pi_2((a_{3},\allowbreak{} b)) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))) \land (\pi_1((a_{3},\allowbreak{} b)) = x_{1} \land \pi_2((a_{3},\allowbreak{} b)) = y))\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((\exists x_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \times \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; x_{2} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x_{2}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x_{2})) = \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} x_{1},\allowbreak{} y)) = (\exists a_{3}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \exists b\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (\pi_1((a_{3},\allowbreak{} b)) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land \pi_2((a_{3},\allowbreak{} b)) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))) \land (\pi_1((a_{3},\allowbreak{} b)) = x_{1} \land \pi_2((a_{3},\allowbreak{} b)) = y))\).
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(a_{3}\) in \(\operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\).
By \paperlink{premise:6578697374735f65715f72696768745f7269676874}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6578697374735f65715f72696768745f7269676874}} with \(\alpha = \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\), we obtain \((\exists a_{4}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (a_{5} \mapsto a_{3} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land a_{5} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2})))(a_{4}) \land ((a_{5} \mapsto a_{3} = x_{1})(a_{4}) \land a_{4} = y)) \iff (a_{4} \mapsto a_{3} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land a_{4} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2})))(y) \land (a_{4} \mapsto a_{3} = x_{1})(y)\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\exists a_{4}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (a_{3} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land a_{4} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))) \land (a_{3} = x_{1} \land a_{4} = y)) = ((a_{3} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land y \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))) \land a_{3} = x_{1})\).
Applying the hypothesis \(\forall n_{1}\, :\, \mathbb{N},\; y \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(n_{1}) \iff \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(y) = n_{1}\), we obtain \(y \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2})) \iff \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(y) = \pi_2(a_{2})\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((y \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))) = (\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(y) = \pi_2(a_{2}))\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \(((a_{3} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land y \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))) \land a_{3} = x_{1}) = ((a_{3} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(y) = \pi_2(a_{2})) \land a_{3} = x_{1})\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((\exists a_{4}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (a_{3} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land a_{4} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))) \land (a_{3} = x_{1} \land a_{4} = y)) = ((a_{3} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(y) = \pi_2(a_{2})) \land a_{3} = x_{1})\).
\end{proofstep}
By function extensionality, we obtain \((x_{2} \mapsto \exists a_{3}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (x_{2} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land a_{3} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))) \land (x_{2} = x_{1} \land a_{3} = y)) = (x_{2} \mapsto (x_{2} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(y) = \pi_2(a_{2})) \land x_{2} = x_{1})\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((\exists x_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \exists a_{3}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (x_{2} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land a_{3} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))) \land (x_{2} = x_{1} \land a_{3} = y)) = (\exists x_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (x_{2} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(y) = \pi_2(a_{2})) \land x_{2} = x_{1})\).
By \paperlink{premise:6578697374735f65715f7269676874}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6578697374735f65715f7269676874}} with \(\alpha = \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\), we obtain \((\exists a_{3}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (a_{4} \mapsto a_{4} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(y) = \pi_2(a_{2}))(a_{3}) \land a_{3} = x_{1}) \iff (a_{3} \mapsto a_{3} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(y) = \pi_2(a_{2}))(x_{1})\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\exists a_{3}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (a_{3} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(y) = \pi_2(a_{2})) \land a_{3} = x_{1}) = (x_{1} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(y) = \pi_2(a_{2}))\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((\exists x_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \exists a_{3}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (x_{2} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land a_{3} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))) \land (x_{2} = x_{1} \land a_{3} = y)) = (x_{1} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(y) = \pi_2(a_{2}))\).
Applying the hypothesis \(\forall n_{1}\, :\, \mathbb{N},\; x_{1} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(n_{1}) \iff \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x_{1}) = n_{1}\), we obtain \(x_{1} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \iff \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x_{1}) = \pi_1(a_{2})\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((x_{1} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2}))) = (\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x_{1}) = \pi_1(a_{2}))\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((x_{1} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(y) = \pi_2(a_{2})) = (\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x_{1}) = \pi_1(a_{2}) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(y) = \pi_2(a_{2}))\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((\exists x_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \exists a_{3}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (x_{2} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})) \land a_{3} \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))) \land (x_{2} = x_{1} \land a_{3} = y)) = (\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x_{1}) = \pi_1(a_{2}) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(y) = \pi_2(a_{2}))\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((\exists x_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \times \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; x_{2} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x_{2}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x_{2})) = \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} x_{1},\allowbreak{} y)) = (\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x_{1}) = \pi_1(a_{2}) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(y) = \pi_2(a_{2}))\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((\operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} x_{1},\allowbreak{} y) \in \operatorname{map}(\operatorname{Function\mathord{:}Embedding\mathord{:}mk}(x_{2} \mapsto \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x_{2}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x_{2}))),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))))) = (\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x_{1}) = \pi_1(a_{2}) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(y) = \pi_2(a_{2}))\).
By congruence, we obtain \((a_{2} \in \operatorname{antidiagonal}(n_{2}) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} x_{1},\allowbreak{} y) \in \operatorname{map}(\operatorname{Function\mathord{:}Embedding\mathord{:}mk}(x_{2} \mapsto \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(x_{2}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(x_{2}))),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{2})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{2}))))) = (\pi_1(a_{2}) + \pi_2(a_{2}) = n_{2} \land (\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x_{1}) = \pi_1(a_{2}) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(y) = \pi_2(a_{2})))\).
\end{proofstep}
The substitution uses the following argument.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(a_{2}\) in \(\mathbb{N}\).
By \paperlink{premise:6578697374735f65715f72696768745f726967687427}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6578697374735f65715f72696768745f726967687427}} with \(\alpha = \mathbb{N}\), we obtain \((\exists a_{3}\, :\, \mathbb{N},\; (a_{4} \mapsto a_{2} + a_{4} = n_{2})(a_{3}) \land ((a_{4} \mapsto \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x_{1}) = a_{2})(a_{3}) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(y) = a_{3})) \iff (a_{3} \mapsto a_{2} + a_{3} = n_{2})(\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(y)) \land (a_{3} \mapsto \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x_{1}) = a_{2})(\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(y))\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\exists a_{3}\, :\, \mathbb{N},\; a_{2} + a_{3} = n_{2} \land (\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x_{1}) = a_{2} \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(y) = a_{3})) = (a_{2} + \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(y) = n_{2} \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x_{1}) = a_{2})\).
\end{proofstep}
Rewriting with \paperlink{theorem:10}{Lemma~\ref{theorem:10}}, \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f6269556e696f6e}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f6269556e696f6e}}, \paperlink{premise:66756e657874}{\emph{Function extensionality}} (\paperlink{premise:66756e657874}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:66756e657874}}), \paperlink{premise:50726f642e657869737473}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:50726f642e657869737473}}, \paperlink{premise:6578697374735f65715f726967687427}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6578697374735f65715f726967687427}}, \paperlink{premise:4e61742e6164645f6c6566745f696e6a}{\emph{Alias}} (\paperlink{premise:4e61742e6164645f6c6566745f696e6a}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e61742e6164645f6c6566745f696e6a}}), \paperlink{premise:6966665f73656c66}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6966665f73656c66}} and the two identities established above gives \(\operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} x_{1},\allowbreak{} y) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(n_{2} + 1) \iff \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x_{1}) + \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(y) + 1 = n_{2} + 1\).
Use the hypothesis \(n_{1} = n_{2} + 1\).
Reversing the equality, we obtain \(n_{2} + 1 = n_{1}\).
Substituting equals, we obtain \(\operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} x_{1},\allowbreak{} y) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(n_{1}) \iff \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(\operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} x_{1},\allowbreak{} y)) = n_{1}\).
\end{proofstep}
By definition, we obtain \(n_{1} = n_{1}\).
The zero and successor cases exhaust the natural numbers. Hence \(\operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} x_{1},\allowbreak{} y) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(n_{1}) \iff \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(\operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} x_{1},\allowbreak{} y)) = n_{1}\).
\end{proofstep}
By \paperlink{premise:547265652e756e69745265634f6e}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:547265652e756e69745265634f6e}} with \(t = x\), we obtain \(x \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}\left(n\right) \iff \operatorname{num\,Nodes}\left(x\right) = n\).
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}\label{theorem:12}
Let \(x\) be an element of \(\operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\). Then
\begin{center}
\(\displaystyle x \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x)).\)
\end{center}
\end{corollary}

\begin{proof}
By \paperlink{theorem:11}{Lemma~\ref{theorem:11}}, we obtain \(x \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x)) \iff \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x) = \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x)\).
By definition, we obtain \(\operatorname{num\,Nodes}\left(x\right) = \operatorname{num\,Nodes}\left(x\right)\).
By the reverse implication, we obtain \(x \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}\left(\operatorname{num\,Nodes}\left(x\right)\right)\).
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}\label{theorem:13}
Let \(n\) be a natural number. Then
\begin{center}
\(\displaystyle \operatorname{underlying\,set}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(n)) = \{x\in \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \mid \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x) = n\}.\)
\end{center}
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
The substitution uses the following argument.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(x\) in \(\operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\).
By \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f636f65}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f636f65}} with \(\alpha = \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\), we obtain \(x \in \operatorname{underlying\,set}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(n)) \iff x \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(n)\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((x \in \operatorname{underlying\,set}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(n))) = (x \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(n))\).
By \paperlink{theorem:11}{Lemma~\ref{theorem:11}}, we obtain \(x \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}\left(n\right) \iff \operatorname{num\,Nodes}\left(x\right) = n\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((x \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(n)) = (\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x) = n)\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((x \in \operatorname{underlying\,set}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(n))) = (\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x) = n)\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((x \in \operatorname{underlying\,set}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(n)) \iff \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x) = n) = (\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x) = n \iff \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x) = n)\).
By \paperlink{premise:6966665f73656c66}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6966665f73656c66}} with \(p = \operatorname{num\,Nodes}\left(x\right) = n\), we obtain \((\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x) = n \iff \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x) = n) = \mathrm{True}\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((x \in \operatorname{underlying\,set}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(n)) \iff \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x) = n) = \mathrm{True}\).
\end{proofstep}
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:666f72616c6c5f636f6e6772}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:666f72616c6c5f636f6e6772}}, \paperlink{premise:696d706c6965735f74727565}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:696d706c6965735f74727565}} and the identity established above gives \(\forall a\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; a \in \operatorname{underlying\,set}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(n)) \iff \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(a) = n\).
By \paperlink{premise:5365742e657874}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:5365742e657874}} with \(\alpha = \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\), we obtain \(\operatorname{underlying\,set}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(n)) = \{x\in \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}) \mid \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x) = n\}\).
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[{The Catalan count for binary trees with a fixed number of nodes}]\label{theorem:14}
Let \(n\) be a natural number. Then
\begin{center}
\(\displaystyle |\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(n)| = \operatorname{catalan}(n).\)
\end{center}
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Proceed by strong induction on \(n\).
\emph{Base case.}
\begin{proofstep}
Rewriting with \paperlink{theorem:9}{Lemma~\ref{theorem:9}}, \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f73696e676c65746f6e}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f73696e676c65746f6e}} and \paperlink{theorem:1}{Lemma~\ref{theorem:1}} gives \(\left|\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}\left(0\right)\right| = \operatorname{catalan}\left(0\right)\).
\end{proofstep}
\emph{Successor step.}
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(n_{1}\) in \(\mathbb{N}\).
Assume as induction hypothesis that \(\forall m\, :\, \mathbb{N},\; m \le n_{1} \implies |\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(m)| = \operatorname{catalan}(m)\).
The substitution uses the following argument.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(x\) in \(\mathbb{N} \times \mathbb{N}\).
By definition, we obtain \((x \in \operatorname{underlying\,set}(\operatorname{antidiagonal}(n_{1}))) = (x \in \operatorname{underlying\,set}(\operatorname{antidiagonal}(n_{1})))\).
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(y\) in \(\mathbb{N} \times \mathbb{N}\).
By definition, we obtain \((y \in \operatorname{underlying\,set}(\operatorname{antidiagonal}(n_{1}))) = (y \in \operatorname{underlying\,set}(\operatorname{antidiagonal}(n_{1})))\).
By definition, we obtain \(\left(x \ne y\right) = \left(x \ne y\right)\).
By \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6469736a6f696e745f6c656674}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6469736a6f696e745f6c656674}} with \(\alpha = \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\), we obtain \(\operatorname{Disjoint}((\mathit{ij} \mapsto \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(\mathit{ij})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(\mathit{ij}))))(x),\allowbreak{} (\mathit{ij} \mapsto \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(\mathit{ij})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(\mathit{ij}))))(y)) \iff (\forall a\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; a \in (\mathit{ij} \mapsto \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(\mathit{ij})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(\mathit{ij}))))(x) \implies a \notin (\mathit{ij} \mapsto \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(\mathit{ij})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(\mathit{ij}))))(y))\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \(\operatorname{Disjoint}(\operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(x)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(x))),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(y)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(y)))) = (\forall a\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; a \in \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(x)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(x))) \implies a \notin \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(y)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(y))))\).
By \paperlink{premise:696d706c6965735f636f6e6772}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:696d706c6965735f636f6e6772}} with \(p_{1} = x \ne y\) and \(p_{2} = x \ne y\) and \(q_{1} = \operatorname{Disjoint}((\mathit{ij} \mapsto \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(\mathit{ij})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(\mathit{ij}))))(x),\allowbreak{} (\mathit{ij} \mapsto \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(\mathit{ij})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(\mathit{ij}))))(y))\) and \(q_{2} = \forall a\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; a \in \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(x)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(x))) \implies a \notin \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(y)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(y)))\), we obtain \((x \ne y \implies \operatorname{Disjoint}(\operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(x)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(x))),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(y)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(y))))) = (x \ne y \implies (\forall a_{1}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; a_{1} \in \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(x)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(x))) \implies a_{1} \notin \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(y)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(y)))))\).
By \paperlink{premise:696d706c6965735f636f6e6772}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:696d706c6965735f636f6e6772}} with \(p_{1} = y \in \operatorname{underlying\,set}(\operatorname{antidiagonal}\left(n_{1}\right))\) and \(p_{2} = y \in \operatorname{underlying\,set}(\operatorname{antidiagonal}\left(n_{1}\right))\) and \(q_{1} = x \ne y \implies \operatorname{Disjoint}(\operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(x)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(x))),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(y)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(y))))\) and \(q_{2} = x \ne y \implies (\forall a_{1}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; a_{1} \in \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(x)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(x))) \implies a_{1} \notin \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(y)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(y))))\), we obtain \((y \in \operatorname{underlying\,set}(\operatorname{antidiagonal}(n_{1})) \implies x \ne y \implies \operatorname{Disjoint}(\operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(x)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(x))),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(y)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(y))))) = (y \in \operatorname{underlying\,set}(\operatorname{antidiagonal}(n_{1})) \implies x \ne y \implies (\forall a_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; a_{2} \in \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(x)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(x))) \implies a_{2} \notin \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(y)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(y)))))\).
\end{proofstep}
By \paperlink{premise:666f72616c6c5f636f6e6772}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:666f72616c6c5f636f6e6772}} with \(\alpha = \mathbb{N} \times \mathbb{N}\), we obtain \((\forall a\, :\, \mathbb{N} \times \mathbb{N},\; a \in \operatorname{underlying\,set}(\operatorname{antidiagonal}(n_{1})) \implies x \ne a \implies \operatorname{Disjoint}(\operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(x)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(x))),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a))))) = (\forall a\, :\, \mathbb{N} \times \mathbb{N},\; a \in \operatorname{underlying\,set}(\operatorname{antidiagonal}(n_{1})) \implies x \ne a \implies (\forall a_{3}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; a_{3} \in \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(x)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(x))) \implies a_{3} \notin \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a)))))\).
By \paperlink{premise:696d706c6965735f636f6e6772}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:696d706c6965735f636f6e6772}} with \(p_{1} = x \in \operatorname{underlying\,set}(\operatorname{antidiagonal}\left(n_{1}\right))\) and \(p_{2} = x \in \operatorname{underlying\,set}(\operatorname{antidiagonal}\left(n_{1}\right))\) and \(q_{1} = \forall a\, :\, \mathbb{N} \times \mathbb{N},\; a \in \operatorname{underlying\,set}(\operatorname{antidiagonal}(n_{1})) \implies x \ne a \implies \operatorname{Disjoint}(\operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(x)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(x))),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a))))\) and \(q_{2} = \forall a\, :\, \mathbb{N} \times \mathbb{N},\; a \in \operatorname{underlying\,set}(\operatorname{antidiagonal}(n_{1})) \implies x \ne a \implies (\forall a_{3}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; a_{3} \in \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(x)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(x))) \implies a_{3} \notin \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a))))\), we obtain \((x \in \operatorname{underlying\,set}(\operatorname{antidiagonal}(n_{1})) \implies (\forall a_{1}\, :\, \mathbb{N} \times \mathbb{N},\; a_{1} \in \operatorname{underlying\,set}(\operatorname{antidiagonal}(n_{1})) \implies x \ne a_{1} \implies \operatorname{Disjoint}(\operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(x)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(x))),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1})))))) = (x \in \operatorname{underlying\,set}(\operatorname{antidiagonal}(n_{1})) \implies (\forall a_{1}\, :\, \mathbb{N} \times \mathbb{N},\; a_{1} \in \operatorname{underlying\,set}(\operatorname{antidiagonal}(n_{1})) \implies x \ne a_{1} \implies (\forall a_{4}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; a_{4} \in \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(x)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(x))) \implies a_{4} \notin \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(a_{1})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(a_{1}))))))\).

The substitution uses the following argument.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(x\) in \(\mathbb{N} \times \mathbb{N}\).
Assume \(x \in \operatorname{underlying\,set}(\operatorname{antidiagonal}\left(n_{1}\right))\).
Fix \(y\) in \(\mathbb{N} \times \mathbb{N}\).
Assume \(y \in \operatorname{underlying\,set}(\operatorname{antidiagonal}\left(n_{1}\right))\).
Assume \(x \ne y\).
Fix \(a_{3}\) in \(\operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\).
Assume \(a_{3} \in \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(x)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(x)))\).
The substitution uses the following argument.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(a_{5}\) in \(\operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right) \times \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\).
By \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f70726f64756374}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f70726f64756374}} with \(\alpha = \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\) and \(\beta = \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\), we obtain \(a_{5} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(y)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(y))) \iff \pi_1(a_{5}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(y)) \land \pi_2(a_{5}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(y))\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((a_{5} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(y)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(y)))) = (\pi_1(a_{5}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(y)) \land \pi_2(a_{5}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(y)))\).
By \paperlink{theorem:11}{Lemma~\ref{theorem:11}}, we obtain \(\pi_1(a_{5}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(y)) \iff \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(\pi_1(a_{5})) = \pi_1(y)\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\pi_1(a_{5}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(y))) = (\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(\pi_1(a_{5})) = \pi_1(y))\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((y_{1} \mapsto \pi_1(a_{5}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(y)) \land y_{1}) = (y_{1} \mapsto \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(\pi_1(a_{5})) = \pi_1(y) \land y_{1})\).
By \paperlink{theorem:11}{Lemma~\ref{theorem:11}}, we obtain \(\pi_2(a_{5}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(y)) \iff \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(\pi_2(a_{5})) = \pi_2(y)\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\pi_2(a_{5}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(y))) = (\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(\pi_2(a_{5})) = \pi_2(y))\).
By congruence, we obtain \((\pi_1(a_{5}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(y)) \land \pi_2(a_{5}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(y))) = (\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(\pi_1(a_{5})) = \pi_1(y) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(\pi_2(a_{5})) = \pi_2(y))\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((a_{5} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(y)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(y)))) = (\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(\pi_1(a_{5})) = \pi_1(y) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(\pi_2(a_{5})) = \pi_2(y))\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((a_{5} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(y)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(y))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(a_{5}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(a_{5})) = a_{3}) = ((\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(\pi_1(a_{5})) = \pi_1(y) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(\pi_2(a_{5})) = \pi_2(y)) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(a_{5}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(a_{5})) = a_{3})\).
\end{proofstep}
The substitution uses the following argument.
\begin{proofstep}

By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x_{1}) = \pi_1(y) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x_{2}) = \pi_2(y) \implies \neg (\operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} x_{1},\allowbreak{} x_{2}) = a_{3})) = (\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x_{1}) = \pi_1(y) \implies \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x_{2}) = \pi_2(y) \implies \neg (\operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} x_{1},\allowbreak{} x_{2}) = a_{3}))\).

By \paperlink{premise:666f72616c6c5f636f6e6772}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:666f72616c6c5f636f6e6772}} with \(\alpha = \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\), we obtain \((\forall a_{5}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \neg ((\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x_{1}) = \pi_1(y) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(a_{5}) = \pi_2(y)) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} x_{1},\allowbreak{} a_{5}) = a_{3})) = (\forall a_{5}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x_{1}) = \pi_1(y) \implies \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(a_{5}) = \pi_2(y) \implies \neg (\operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} x_{1},\allowbreak{} a_{5}) = a_{3}))\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((\neg (\exists x_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x_{1}) = \pi_1(y) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x_{2}) = \pi_2(y)) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} x_{1},\allowbreak{} x_{2}) = a_{3})) = (\forall a_{5}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(x_{1}) = \pi_1(y) \implies \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(a_{5}) = \pi_2(y) \implies \neg (\operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} x_{1},\allowbreak{} a_{5}) = a_{3}))\).
