Fix \(x_{1}\) in \(\operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\).
Fix \(x_{2}\) in \(\operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\).
Assume \(\operatorname{num\,Nodes}\left(x_{1}\right) = \pi_1(y)\).
Assume \(\operatorname{num\,Nodes}\left(x_{2}\right) = \pi_2(y)\).
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume as induction hypothesis that \(\forall m\, :\, \mathbb{N},\; m \le \pi_1(y) + \pi_2(y) \implies |\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(m)| = \operatorname{catalan}(m)\).
Assume \(\pi_1(x) + \pi_2(x) = \pi_1(y) + \pi_2(y)\).
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(\mathit{fst}\) in \(\mathbb{N}\).
Fix \(\mathit{snd}\) in \(\mathbb{N}\).
Assume \(\neg \left(\left(\mathit{fst}, \mathit{snd}\right) = y\right)\).
Assume \(\exists a_{10}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \exists b\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(a_{10}) = \pi_1((\mathit{fst},\allowbreak{} \mathit{snd})) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(b) = \pi_2((\mathit{fst},\allowbreak{} \mathit{snd}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} a_{10},\allowbreak{} b) = a_{3}\).
Assume \(\pi_1((\mathit{fst},\allowbreak{} \mathit{snd})) + \pi_2((\mathit{fst},\allowbreak{} \mathit{snd})) = \pi_1(y) + \pi_2(y)\).
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(f\) in \(\mathbb{N}\).
Fix \(s\) in \(\mathbb{N}\).
Assume \(\operatorname{num\,Nodes}\left(x_{1}\right) = \pi_1(\left(f, s\right))\).
Assume \(\operatorname{num\,Nodes}\left(x_{2}\right) = \pi_2(\left(f, s\right))\).
Assume as induction hypothesis that \(\forall m\, :\, \mathbb{N},\; m \le \pi_1((f,\allowbreak{} s)) + \pi_2((f,\allowbreak{} s)) \implies |\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(m)| = \operatorname{catalan}(m)\).
Assume \(\neg \left(\left(\mathit{fst}, \mathit{snd}\right) = \left(f, s\right)\right)\).
Assume \(\pi_1((\mathit{fst},\allowbreak{} \mathit{snd})) + \pi_2((\mathit{fst},\allowbreak{} \mathit{snd})) = \pi_1((f,\allowbreak{} s)) + \pi_2((f,\allowbreak{} s))\).
Use the hypothesis \(\exists a_{10}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \exists b\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(a_{10}) = \pi_1((\mathit{fst},\allowbreak{} \mathit{snd})) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(b) = \pi_2((\mathit{fst},\allowbreak{} \mathit{snd}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} a_{10},\allowbreak{} b) = a_{3}\).
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(w\) in \(\operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\).
Assume \(\exists b\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(w) = \pi_1((\mathit{fst},\allowbreak{} \mathit{snd})) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(b) = \pi_2((\mathit{fst},\allowbreak{} \mathit{snd}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} w,\allowbreak{} b) = a_{3}\).
Use the hypothesis \(\exists b\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(w) = \pi_1((\mathit{fst},\allowbreak{} \mathit{snd})) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(b) = \pi_2((\mathit{fst},\allowbreak{} \mathit{snd}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} w,\allowbreak{} b) = a_{3}\).
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(w_{1}\) in \(\operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\).
Assume \((\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(w) = \pi_1((\mathit{fst},\allowbreak{} \mathit{snd})) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(w_{1}) = \pi_2((\mathit{fst},\allowbreak{} \mathit{snd}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} w,\allowbreak{} w_{1}) = a_{3}\).
Use the hypothesis \((\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(w) = \pi_1((\mathit{fst},\allowbreak{} \mathit{snd})) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(w_{1}) = \pi_2((\mathit{fst},\allowbreak{} \mathit{snd}))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} w,\allowbreak{} w_{1}) = a_{3}\).
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(w) = \pi_1((\mathit{fst},\allowbreak{} \mathit{snd})) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(w_{1}) = \pi_2((\mathit{fst},\allowbreak{} \mathit{snd}))\).

Substituting equals, we obtain \(\neg \left(\operatorname{node}\left(\operatorname{unit}, x_{1}, x_{2}\right) = a_{3}\right)\).
\end{proofstep}

The substitution uses the following argument.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(a_{8}\) in \(\operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right) \times \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\).
By \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f70726f64756374}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f70726f64756374}} with \(\alpha = \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\) and \(\beta = \operatorname{Tree}\left(\operatorname{Unit}\right)\), we obtain \(a_{8} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(x)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(x))) \iff \pi_1(a_{8}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(x)) \land \pi_2(a_{8}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(x))\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((a_{8} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(x)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(x)))) = (\pi_1(a_{8}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(x)) \land \pi_2(a_{8}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(x)))\).
By \paperlink{theorem:11}{Lemma~\ref{theorem:11}}, we obtain \(\pi_1(a_{8}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(x)) \iff \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(\pi_1(a_{8})) = \pi_1(x)\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\pi_1(a_{8}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(x))) = (\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(\pi_1(a_{8})) = \pi_1(x))\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((y_{1} \mapsto \pi_1(a_{8}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(x)) \land y_{1}) = (y_{1} \mapsto \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(\pi_1(a_{8})) = \pi_1(x) \land y_{1})\).
By \paperlink{theorem:11}{Lemma~\ref{theorem:11}}, we obtain \(\pi_2(a_{8}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(x)) \iff \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(\pi_2(a_{8})) = \pi_2(x)\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\pi_2(a_{8}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(x))) = (\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(\pi_2(a_{8})) = \pi_2(x))\).
By congruence, we obtain \((\pi_1(a_{8}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(x)) \land \pi_2(a_{8}) \in \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(x))) = (\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(\pi_1(a_{8})) = \pi_1(x) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(\pi_2(a_{8})) = \pi_2(x))\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((a_{8} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(x)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(x)))) = (\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(\pi_1(a_{8})) = \pi_1(x) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(\pi_2(a_{8})) = \pi_2(x))\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((a_{8} \in \operatorname{sprod}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(x)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(x))) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(a_{8}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(a_{8})) = a_{3}) = ((\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(\pi_1(a_{8})) = \pi_1(x) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(\pi_2(a_{8})) = \pi_2(x)) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} \pi_1(a_{8}),\allowbreak{} \pi_2(a_{8})) = a_{3})\).
\end{proofstep}

Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f6d6170}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f6d6170}}, \paperlink{premise:66756e657874}{\emph{Function extensionality}} (\paperlink{premise:66756e657874}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:66756e657874}}), \paperlink{premise:50726f642e657869737473}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:50726f642e657869737473}} and the identity established above in the preceding result gives \(\exists a_{8}\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; \exists b\, :\, \operatorname{Tree}(\operatorname{Unit}),\; (\operatorname{num\,Nodes}(a_{8}) = \pi_1(x) \land \operatorname{num\,Nodes}(b) = \pi_2(x)) \land \operatorname{node}(\operatorname{unit},\allowbreak{} a_{8},\allowbreak{} b) = a_{3}\).

By definition, we obtain \(\operatorname{antidiagonal}\left(n_{1}\right) = \operatorname{antidiagonal}\left(n_{1}\right)\).
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(x\) in \(\mathbb{N} \times \mathbb{N}\).
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(i\) in \(\mathbb{N}\).
Fix \(j\) in \(\mathbb{N}\).
Assume \(\left(i, j\right) \in \operatorname{antidiagonal}\left(n_{1}\right)\).
Rewriting with \(\forall m\, :\, \mathbb{N},\; m \le n_{1} \implies |\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(m)| = \operatorname{catalan}(m)\), \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e616e7469646961676f6e616c2e736e645f6c65}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e616e7469646961676f6e616c2e736e645f6c65}} (using \(\left(i, j\right) \in \operatorname{antidiagonal}\left(n_{1}\right)\)), \paperlink{premise:4e61742e696e737443616e6f6e6963616c6c794f726465726564416464}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e61742e696e737443616e6f6e6963616c6c794f726465726564416464}}, \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e616e7469646961676f6e616c2e6673745f6c65}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e616e7469646961676f6e616c2e6673745f6c65}} (using \(\left(i, j\right) \in \operatorname{antidiagonal}\left(n_{1}\right)\)), \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f70726f64756374}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f70726f64756374}} and \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f6d6170}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f6d6170}} gives \(|\operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1((i,\allowbreak{} j))),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2((i,\allowbreak{} j))))| = \operatorname{catalan}(\pi_1((i,\allowbreak{} j))) \cdot \operatorname{catalan}(\pi_2((i,\allowbreak{} j)))\).
\end{proofstep}
Every ordered pair has two components. Hence \(x \in \operatorname{antidiagonal}(n_{1}) \implies |\operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(x)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(x)))| = \operatorname{catalan}(\pi_1(x)) \cdot \operatorname{catalan}(\pi_2(x))\).
\end{proofstep}
By \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e73756d5f636f6e6772}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e73756d5f636f6e6772}} with \(\iota = \mathbb{N} \times \mathbb{N}\) and \(M = \mathbb{N}\), we obtain \(\sum_{u\in \operatorname{antidiagonal}(n_{1})}(|\operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(u)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(u)))|) = \sum_{\mathit{ij}\in \operatorname{antidiagonal}(n_{1})}(\operatorname{catalan}(\pi_1(\mathit{ij})) \cdot \operatorname{catalan}(\pi_2(\mathit{ij})))\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{theorem:3}{Lemma~\ref{theorem:3}} in the preceding result gives \(\sum_{u\in \operatorname{antidiagonal}(n_{1})}(|\operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(u)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(u)))|) = \operatorname{catalan}(n_{1} + 1)\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f6269556e696f6e}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f6269556e696f6e}}, \paperlink{premise:666f72616c6c5f636f6e6772}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:666f72616c6c5f636f6e6772}}, the identity established above and the auxiliary result established above in the preceding result gives \(|\bigcup_{\mathit{ij}\in \operatorname{antidiagonal}(n_{1})} \operatorname{pairwise\,Node}(\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_1(\mathit{ij})),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(\pi_2(\mathit{ij})))| = \operatorname{catalan}(n_{1} + 1)\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{theorem:10}{Lemma~\ref{theorem:10}} in the preceding result gives \(|\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}(n_{1} + 1)| = \operatorname{catalan}(n_{1} + 1)\).
\end{proofstep}
By strong induction, \(\left|\operatorname{trees\,Of\,Num\,Nodes\,Eq}\left(n\right)\right| = \operatorname{catalan}\left(n\right)\).
\end{proof}
